## Frozen native profile and ordinary-player tools (2026-10-01)

Generic ownership remains in this authoritative repository. Added closed,
profile-bound bootstrap and ordinary pause/resume/speed/event/checkpoint MCP
consumers, and an independent read-only 1.20 clock using process access 0x0410.
No caller PID, artifact, shell, key, coordinate or session identity is accepted.
The existing native provider supplies ordinary commands; no XAR rule is invented
and no one-life controller, observer, date jump or tick skip is started.
Exact process/build/offline/lease/userdir guards run before and after reads/actions;
nonempty isolated crashes deny even if the process still exists during exception
handling. Native and save-byte postconditions are mandatory; failed ACKs retain RED.
30 deterministic clock/profile/ordinary-player/semantic tests pass. Companion
core-only R6 exercised the read-only clock before its later unbridged crash; that
is neither live attachment qualification nor a century gameplay result. Calendar
conversion remains unbound and needs independent annual save metadata.
See [bootstrap contract](../../ck3-native-ai/frozen-native-profile-bootstrap-2026-10-01.md).
Native CMake fixture/build qualification remains a separate work package.


## Native campaign projection without episode policy (2026-10-01)

Ownership review found that the existing native driver's default snapshot path
binds a legacy one-life identity even without its controller. Added an explicit
`episode_projection="native_campaign"` constructor mode and made the frozen
profile consumer require that marker in readback. It follows actual engine
played-character frames through death and succession, creates no episode/campaign
goal, and rejects supplied lifecycle/game-rule bindings. Default one-life callers
retain their behavior. Ordinary MCP tools still expose only the existing narrow
pause/resume/speed/event/save operations.
34 clock/profile/campaign-projection/semantic tests and five relevant existing
native-driver regression tests pass. The four new mode tests use the real native
provider with its existing fake transport, including event selection after a
natural player switch; they are fixture evidence, not live succession acceptance.
No desktop input or DLL attachment was performed. See

Specific campaign dates/results and private raw artifacts stay suite. Docs-only diff check; no new tests, tool/native/policy changes or live operation. The proposed cold load was not executed; future seeding retains its unique named-SHA gate.

并行工作仍为有效独立存档链的唯一operator、实际战争结算只读审计、专属最小源修复与通用文档富化，未抢占游戏或改有效冻结树。下一门槛为新源正常保存冷续后的实际奖励/原业务读回；文档交付按master普通push及exact官方CI另记终态。

## 跨 mod 富化：CB 原选择先于列表变动（2026-10-02）

[既有CB作用域知识](../../grammar/pitfalls.md#cb-原目标的基数与保存位置2026-10-02)补充完整CB头部的单目标基数合同、combine_into_one的UI含义、原选中title在列表变动前保存与consumer间重写追踪。两份当前原版文件hash及真实原版保存示例闭合；一次出处/旧文档反向字节/新增链接检查通过。本包仅native-source/static-supported，tooltip保存顺序、实际朝贡/头衔结算与弱引用均engine NOT_RUN；无CK3/script执行，open_kaishek not-applicable。专用四行修复及原64条tooltip诊断留独立仓，没有第三方代码/资产/存档复制或新的通用实现。

并行工作继续沿有效存档链正常保存后冷续；源码最小补丁已独立入库，通用文档在boot/保存等待外推进，不占屏幕也不改有效冻结工具。MCP/OODA及百年readiness未提升；下一门槛为exact源码实际回调/预览与完整业务后置读回，以及本doc commit的官方CI终态。
